<script>
    requirejs([
        'jquery',
        'algoliaBundle',
        'htmlEntities',
        'mage/utils/wrapper',
        'Convert_Norli/js/gtm/search-results'
    ], function($, algoliaBundle, htmlEntities, wrapper, gtmSearchResults) {
        var ecommObjectInited = null;

        if (window.algoliaConfig.request.query.length > 0) {
            window.algoliaConfig.request.query = window.htmlentities.decode(algoliaConfig.request.query);
        }

        algolia.registerHook("beforeWidgetInitialization", function (allWidgetConfiguration) {
            if(!window.algoliaConfig.instant.infiniteScrollEnabled){
                allWidgetConfiguration.pagination = $.extend(
                    allWidgetConfiguration.pagination, {
                        showFirstLast: false,
                        cssClasses: {
                            disabled: 'disabled'
                        }
                    });
            }

            allWidgetConfiguration.analytics = {
                pushFunction: function (formattedParameters, state, results) {
                    var ecommObject = window.dataLayer.filter(function(item) {
                        return item.hasOwnProperty('ecomm_pagetype')
                    });

                    if (ecommObject.length) {
                        ecommObject[0].ecomm_pagetype = 'searchresults';
                    }
                }
            }

            return allWidgetConfiguration;
        });

        algolia.registerHook("beforeInstantsearchInit", function (instantsearchOptions) {
            if (!ecommObjectInited && window.dataLayer) {

                ecommObjectInited = window.dataLayer.filter(function (item) {
                    return item.hasOwnProperty('ecomm_pagetype');
                })

                if (ecommObjectInited.length) {
                    ecommObjectInited = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ecommObjectInited[0]));
                }

                if (typeof instantsearchOptions.searchFunction === 'function') {
                    instantsearchOptions.searchFunction = wrapper.wrap(instantsearchOptions.searchFunction, function (originalAction, helper) {
                        var ecommObject = window.dataLayer.filter(function(item) {
                            return item.hasOwnProperty('ecomm_pagetype')
                        });

                        originalAction(helper);

                        if (helper.state.query === '' && !algoliaConfig.isSearchPage) {
                            if (ecommObject.length) {
                                ecommObject[0].ecomm_pagetype = ecommObjectInited.ecomm_pagetype;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
            }

            return instantsearchOptions;
        });

        algolia.registerHook("afterInstantsearchStart", function(search) {
            var gtmSearchResultsTimeoutHandler = null;

            search.on('render', function () {
                if (gtmSearchResultsTimeoutHandler) {
                    clearTimeout(gtmSearchResultsTimeoutHandler);
                }

                gtmSearchResultsTimeoutHandler = setTimeout(function () {
                    gtmSearchResultsTimeoutHandler = null;
                    gtmSearchResults();
                }, 500);
            });

            return search;
        });
    });
</script>

    requirejs([
        'jquery',
        'algoliaBundle',
        'algoliaInfinitiScrollWidget'
    ], function($, algoliaBundle, infiniteScrollWidget) {
        var isNewAPI = (typeof algolia !== 'undefined' && 
                        typeof algolia.registerHook === 'function'),
            instantConfig = algoliaConfig.instant,
            beforeWidgetInitialization;

        beforeWidgetInitialization = function(allWidgetConfiguration) {
            if (instantConfig.infiniteScrollEnabled) {
                var removeWidgets = ['hits', 'pagination'],
                    alogliaInfiniteConfig; 
                if (instantConfig.infiniteScrollAuto && allWidgetConfiguration['infiniteHits'] !== undefined) {
                    alogliaInfiniteConfig = allWidgetConfiguration['infiniteHits'];
                    removeWidgets.push('infiniteHits');
                }

                removeWidgets.forEach(function (widgetKey) {
                    if (allWidgetConfiguration[widgetKey]) {
                        delete allWidgetConfiguration[widgetKey];
                    }
                });
 
                //Infinite scroll
                if (instantConfig.infiniteScrollAuto) {
                    allWidgetConfiguration.custom.unshift(
                        infiniteScrollWidget({
                            container: '#instant-search-results-container',
                            templates: {
                                item: $('#instant-hit-template').html(),
                                empty: algoliaConfig.translations.noResults
                            },
                            transformData: alogliaInfiniteConfig.transformData,
                            hitsPerPage: algoliaConfig.hitsPerPage,
                            offset: instantConfig.infiniteScrollOffset
                        })
                    );

                    allWidgetConfiguration.custom.push({
                        render: function (data) {
                            if (window.SgyIAS !== undefined) {
                                setTimeout(function(){
                                    if (data.results.query.length > 0 && location.hash.length < 1) {
                                       window.SgyIAS.destroy();
                                    } else {
                                       //window.SgyIAS.init();
                                       $.tinyEqualizer.reflow();
                                    }
                                }, 500);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
            }

            return allWidgetConfiguration;
        }
        

        if (isNewAPI) {
            algolia.registerHook("beforeWidgetInitialization", beforeWidgetInitialization);
        } else {
            var oldWidgetInitialization;

            if (typeof algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization === 'function') {
                oldWidgetInitialization = algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization;
            }

            algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization = function(allWidgetConfiguration) {
                if (typeof oldWidgetInitialization == 'function') {
                    allWidgetConfiguration = oldWidgetInitialization(allWidgetConfiguration);
                }

                return beforeWidgetInitialization(allWidgetConfiguration);
            };
        }
    });

    requirejs(['algoliaBundle'],function(algoliaBundle) {
        var oldWidgetInitialization,
            swatchTypeClass = ['text','color','image'],
            $ = algoliaBundle.$;

        if (window.algoliaConfig.swatches_config) {
            if (typeof algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization === 'function') {
                oldWidgetInitialization = algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization;
            }

            algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization = function(allWidgetConfiguration) {
                if (typeof oldWidgetInitialization == 'function') {
                    allWidgetConfiguration = oldWidgetInitialization(allWidgetConfiguration);
                } 
                customAttributeFacet = {};
                if (allWidgetConfiguration.refinementList) {
                    var swatchTemplate = algoliaBundle.Hogan.compile($('#refinements-swatch-item-template').html()); 

                    $.each(allWidgetConfiguration.refinementList, function(key, config) {
                        if (window.algoliaConfig.swatches_config[config.attributeName] !== undefined) {
                            var swatchesConfig = window.algoliaConfig.swatches_config[config.attributeName];

                            config.cssClasses.root += ' swatches has-' + swatchesConfig.display_mode;
                            config.templates.item = (swatchesConfig.display_mode === 'images-labels')
                                ? $('#refinements-swatch-image-label-item-template').html()
                                : $('#refinements-swatch-default-item-template').html(); 
                            
                            config.transformData = function(item) {
                                //Added support fo InstantSearch V2
                                var value = item.value ? item.value : item.name,
                                    optionData = swatchesConfig.options[value];

                                item.value = value;
                                if (optionData) {
                                    item = $.extend(item, optionData);
                                    item.swatchTypeClass = swatchTypeClass[optionData.type];
                                    item.swatch = swatchTemplate.render(item);
                                }

                                return item;
                            }

                            allWidgetConfiguration.refinementList[key] = config;
                        }
                    });
                }

                return allWidgetConfiguration;
            }
        }
    });
</script><script>
    requirejs([
        'jquery',
        'mage/utils/wrapper',
        'algoliaBundle',
        'jquery.tinyEqualizer'
    ], function($, wrapper, algoliaBundle) {
        var isNewAPI = (typeof algolia !== 'undefined' && 
                        typeof algolia.registerHook === 'function'),
            beforeWidgetInitialization,
            beforeInstantsearchInit,
            afterInstantsearchInit;

        function handleInputCrossAutocomplete(input) {
            if (input.val().length > 0) {
                input.closest('#algolia-searchbox').find('.clear-query-autocomplete').show();
                input.closest('#algolia-searchbox').find('.magnifying-glass').hide();
            }
            else {
                input.closest('#algolia-searchbox').find('.clear-query-autocomplete').hide();
                input.closest('#algolia-searchbox').find('.magnifying-glass').show();
            }
        }

        beforeWidgetInitialization = function(allWidgetConfiguration) {
            if (!algoliaConfig.autocomplete.enabled) {
                allWidgetConfiguration.searchBox = $.extend(
                    allWidgetConfiguration.searchBox, {
                        reset: false,
                        magnifier: false
                    }
                );
            }

            return allWidgetConfiguration;
        };

        beforeInstantsearchInit = function(instantsearchOptions) {
            var lastQuery = '';
            if (typeof instantsearchOptions.searchFunction === 'function') {
                instantsearchOptions.searchFunction = wrapper.wrap(instantsearchOptions.searchFunction, function (originalAction, helper) {
                    originalAction(helper);
                    if (helper.state.query !== '') {
                        lastQuery = helper.state.query;
                        if (window.SgyIAS !== undefined) {
                           window.SgyIAS.inited = false;
                           window.SgyIAS.destroy();
                        }   
                    }
                    if (helper.state.query === '' && !algoliaConfig.isSearchPage) {
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            $.tinyEqualizer.reflow();
                            if (lastQuery !== '' && window.SgyIAS !== undefined && !window.SgyIAS.inited) {
                                window.SgyIAS.inited = true;
                                window.SgyIAS.init();
                            }
                        }, 500);
                    }
                });
            }
            return instantsearchOptions;
        };

        afterInstantsearchStart = function(search) {
            search.on('render', function () {
                setTimeout(function(){
                    $.tinyEqualizer.reflow('algolia-product-item-height');
                }, 500);

                if (algoliaConfig.autofocus === false && search.helper.state.query.length === 0 &&  $('input:focus').length === 0) {
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        $('input[autofocus]').focus();
                    }, 100);
                }
            });

            if (!algoliaConfig.autocomplete.enabled) {
                $(document).on('input', algoliaConfig.autocomplete.selector, function () {
                    handleInputCrossAutocomplete($(this));
                });

                if (search.helper.state.query.length !== 0) {
                    handleInputCrossAutocomplete($(algoliaConfig.autocomplete.selector))
                }
            }

            return search;
        };

        if (isNewAPI) {
            algolia.registerHook("beforeWidgetInitialization", beforeWidgetInitialization);

            algolia.registerHook("beforeInstantsearchInit", beforeInstantsearchInit);
            algolia.registerHook("afterInstantsearchStart", afterInstantsearchStart);
        } else {
            var oldWidgetInitialization,
                oldAlgoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit,
                oldAlgoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart;

            if (typeof algoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit === 'function') {
                oldAlgoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit = algoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit;
            }

            if (typeof algoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart === 'function') {
                oldAlgoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart = algoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart;
            }

            if (typeof algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization === 'function') {
                oldWidgetInitialization = algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization;
            }

            algoliaHookBeforeWidgetInitialization = function(allWidgetConfiguration) {
                if (typeof oldWidgetInitialization === 'function') {
                    allWidgetConfiguration = oldWidgetInitialization(allWidgetConfiguration);
                }

                return beforeWidgetInitialization(allWidgetConfiguration);
            };

            algoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit = function(instantsearchOptions) {
                if (typeof oldAlgoliaHookBeforeInstantsearchInit === 'function') {
                    instantsearchOptions = oldAlgoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart(instantsearchOptions);
                }
                if (typeof beforeInstantsearchInit === 'function') {
                    instantsearchOptions = beforeInstantsearchInit(instantsearchOptions);
                }
                return instantsearchOptions;
            };

            algoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart = function(search) {
                if (typeof oldAlgoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart === 'function') {
                    search = oldAlgoliaHookAfterInstantsearchStart(search);
                }

                return afterInstantsearchStart(search);
            };
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    try {
        if (!window.localStorage || !window.sessionStorage) {
            throw new Error();
        }

        localStorage.setItem('storage_test', 1);
        localStorage.removeItem('storage_test');
    } catch(e) {
        (function () {
            var Storage = function (type) {
                var data;

                function createCookie(name, value, days) {
                    var date, expires;

                    if (days) {
                        date = new Date();
                        date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                        expires = '; expires=' + date.toGMTString();
                    } else {
                        expires = '';
                    }
                    document.cookie = name + '=' + value+expires+'; path=/';
                }

                function readCookie(name) {
                    var nameEQ = name + '=',
                        ca = document.cookie.split(';'),
                        i = 0,
                        c;

                    for (i=0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                        c = ca[i];

                        while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
                            c = c.substring(1,c.length);
                        }

                        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
                            return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                        }
                    }

                    return null;
                }

                function setData(data) {
                    data = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data));
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', data, 365);
                }

                function clearData() {
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', '', 365);
                }

                function getData() {
                    var data = type === 'session' ? readCookie(getSessionName()) : readCookie('localStorage');

                    return data ? JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(data)) : {};
                }

                function getSessionName() {
                    if (!window.name) {
                        window.name = new Date().getTime();
                    }

                    return 'sessionStorage' + window.name;
                }

                data = getData();

                return {
                    length: 0,
                    clear: function () {
                        data = {};
                        this.length = 0;
                        clearData();
                    },

                    getItem: function (key) {
                        return data[key] === undefined ? null : data[key];
                    },

                    key: function (i) {
                        var ctr = 0,
                            k;

                        for (k in data) {
                            if (ctr.toString() === i.toString()) {
                                return k;
                            } else {
                                ctr++
                            }
                        }

                        return null;
                    },

                    removeItem: function (key) {
                        delete data[key];
                        this.length--;
                        setData(data);
                    },

                    setItem: function (key, value) {
                        data[key] = value.toString();
                        this.length++;
                        setData(data);
                    }
                };
            };

            window.localStorage.__proto__ = window.localStorage = new Storage('local');
            window.sessionStorage.__proto__ = window.sessionStorage = new Storage('session');
        })();
    }
</script>

            deps: [

            callback: function ($) {
                'use strict';

                var dependencies = [],
                    versionObj;

                $.initNamespaceStorage('mage-translation-storage');
                $.initNamespaceStorage('mage-translation-file-version');
                versionObj = $.localStorage.get('mage-translation-file-version');

                
                if (versionObj.version !== 'fd8f620b0a6261e448edd63e2e38b79cd52ea2e6') {
                    dependencies.push(
                        'text!js-translation.json'
                    );

                }

                require.config({
                    deps: dependencies,
                    callback: function (string) {
                        if (typeof string === 'string') {
                            $.mage.translate.add(JSON.parse(string));
                            $.localStorage.set('mage-translation-storage', string);
                            $.localStorage.set(
                                'mage-translation-file-version',
                                {
                                    version: 'fd8f620b0a6261e448edd63e2e38b79cd52ea2e6'
                                }
                            );
                        } else {
                            $.mage.translate.add($.localStorage.get('mage-translation-storage'));
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });
